42|> Let G be an arbitrary group, and let G = A_1 ∪ … ∪ A_7 be an exact cover by seven disjoint nonempty left cosets of (possibly different) subgroups. Then 25 ≤ |G| ≤ 29. Each admitted order admits an example; no order outside 25..29 admits such a cover. The seven cosets cannot be required to be distinct in advance, and G is not assumed finite in the hypothesis; finiteness and 25..29 follow.

Repeats, differing historical ordinal labels and old statuses are intentional, not new incidents counted twice. Later known events are listed in section3. No reliable campaign-wide kernel-loss TOTAL is asserted.

5|## 1. Material categories that must remain visible
6|
7|- **Forbidden operations:** PA19 workers disclosed writes under `/private/tmp/pa19-p7-d35-drafts` and `/private/tmp/pa19-test8-draft-scratch` (checkpoint039). MOQLS B3row1 author disclosed transient in-memory Python assertions over hand tables despite written-only restrictions (checkpoint051, row1 erratum1 and both reviews): permanent process MAJOR. Assertions are excluded from mathematical evidence. Neither violation is repaired by later written review or erased here.
8|- **Ownership/runtime:** empty/late PID captures, launch identities registered with results, late registry updates, GO-name/owner corrections and delivery polling occurred. CFR21 DFS checked its inner budget too sparsely; a single owned SIGTERM at300.6s produced settled UNKNOWN, not a result. Unqualified supervisor/producer/checker versions had bounded-observation, output-hash/receipt, FD-cleanup, cancellation, cumulative-ceiling and acceptance defects. Source review was not runtime qualification. Native admission was put on HOLD, with only the documented separately qualified GB exception. Earlier receipts retain their actual facts, not a retrospective universal safety guarantee.
9|- **Evidence/preservation:** initial new files not created with explicit open-x; pre-freeze same-path rewrites; copy-then-edit new-version authoring; late read-only freezes; incomplete/estimated clocks/resource captures; wrong abbreviations/hashes/identity labels; byte-identical swapped controls without a semantic mutation; omitted primary excerpts and wrong LF windows. Old wrong files remain, governed by errata.
10|- **Mathematical/claim repairs:** MAJOR proof/interface defects were repaired, not all bookkeeping (Sun closed ledger, CI62/conservative ancestry, DEFORDER; MOLS R1 scope and others). Numerical threshold labels, ray ceilings and no-go/exhaustiveness glosses were corrected. Pre-freeze unwritten arithmetic, drafting replacements, parent route hints and inherited byte estimates were disclosed; strict campaign-wide preregistration is NOT certified. Conditional theorems are not unconditional exclusions.
11|- **Reviews/admission:** real NO_VERDICT attempts are not approvals. Oversize or invalid-budget prompts rejected before launch, and unlaunched superseded prompts, are NOT model NO_VERDICT events. A report-review prompt not actually pasting the claimed v2 text was retired unlaunched. The original217800-byte Sun final Claude packet was also unlaunched, not a failed model review.
12|- **Supervision/cadence:** multiple parent/worker kernel interruptions and checkpoint gaps occurred. Metadata samples are NOT substantive checkpoints. Recovery-clock durations in older gap notes and checkpoint-to-checkpoint intervals below measure different endpoints. The run is NOT certified continuously supervised or cadence-compliant. Worker records include additional Sun/Olson/PA19/RQLS10/MOLS39 late checkpoints, quoted below.
13|- **Bookkeeping/reporting:** wrong Olson p23/p29 providers/ranges propagated into frontier/report drafts; v3+erratum and its literal binding audit govern. FC1023v1 prematurely used ACCEPTED before its second family returned; only the later confirmation accepts it. First report drafts omitted early native science and overstated whole-window HOLD/settlement; they are not final reports. Source-refresh absence claims and fresh-reading attributions are corrected separately.

15|def clock():
16|    return subprocess.check_output(['/bin/date', '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z'], text=True).strip()
17|
18|
19|def write_new(path, value):
20|    with path.open('x') as handle:
21|        json.dump(value, handle, indent=2)
22|        handle.write('\n')
23|        handle.flush()
24|        os.fsync(handle.fileno())
25|
26|
27|def main():
28|    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
29|    parser.add_argument('prompt')
30|    parser.add_argument('--budget-seconds', type=int, default=2400)
31|    args = parser.parse_args()
32|    prompt_path = Path(args.prompt).resolve()
33|    if not prompt_path.is_relative_to(RUN) or not 60 <= args.budget_seconds <= 3600:
34|        raise SystemExit('Invalid scoped prompt or self-budget')
35|    base = prompt_path.with_suffix('')
36|    paths = {key: Path(str(base) + suffix) for key, suffix in
37|             [('stdout', '.stdout.json'), ('stderr', '.stderr.txt'), ('receipt', '.receipt.json'),
38|              ('note', '.note.md'), ('launch', '.launch.json')]}
39|    if any(p.exists() for p in paths.values()):
40|        raise SystemExit('Refusing to overwrite review evidence')
41|    cfg = json.loads(REGISTRY.read_text())['claude']
42|    cmd = ['claude', '--model', cfg['model'], '--effort', cfg['effort'], '--safe-mode', '--no-chrome',
43|           '--tools', '', '--permission-mode', 'dontAsk', '--no-session-persistence',
44|           '--output-format', 'json', '-p']
45|    env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1', TMPDIR=str(RUN / 'control/tmp'),
46|               TMP=str(RUN / 'control/tmp'), TEMP=str(RUN / 'control/tmp'))
47|    begin = time.monotonic()
48|    record = {'started_at': clock(), 'requested_model': cfg['model'], 'requested_effort': cfg['effort'],
49|              'prompt_sha256': hashlib.sha256(prompt_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
50|              'source_sha256': hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(), 'tools': [],
51|              'input_transport': 'stdin_whole_file_single_context', 'prompt_bytes': prompt_path.stat().st_size,
52|              'self_budget_seconds': args.budget_seconds, 'status': 'NO_VERDICT'}
53|    with prompt_path.open('rb') as prompt_input, paths['stdout'].open('x') as stdout, paths['stderr'].open('x') as stderr:
54|        proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=RUN, env=env, stdin=prompt_input,
55|                                stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr, start_new_session=True)
56|        write_new(paths['launch'], dict(record, pid=proc.pid, pgid=os.getpgid(proc.pid), cwd=str(RUN),
57|                                       argv_sha256=hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(cmd).encode()).hexdigest()))
58|        while proc.poll() is None and time.monotonic() - begin < args.budget_seconds:
59|            time.sleep(1)
60|        if proc.poll() is None:
61|            record['stop_requested_at'] = clock()
62|            record['status'] = 'SELF_BUDGET_SIGTERM_NO_VERDICT'
63|            os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
64|            settle_end = time.monotonic() + 60
65|            while proc.poll() is None and time.monotonic() < settle_end:
66|                time.sleep(0.2)
67|            if proc.poll() is None:
68|                record['unsettled_pid'] = proc.pid
69|        record['returncode'] = proc.poll()
70|    if record['returncode'] == 0 and 'stop_requested_at' not in record:
71|        try:
72|            events = json.loads(paths['stdout'].read_text())
73|            if isinstance(events, dict):
74|                events = [events]
75|            finals = [e for e in events if e.get('type') == 'result']
76|            final = finals[-1]
77|            record['reported_init_models'] = sorted({e['model'] for e in events if e.get('type') == 'system' and e.get('model')})
78|            record['reported_model_usage'] = final.get('modelUsage', {})
79|            if final.get('subtype') == 'success' and not final.get('is_error', False):
80|                chunks = [b['text'] for e in events if e.get('type') == 'assistant'
81|                          for b in e.get('message', {}).get('content', [])
82|                          if b.get('type') == 'text' and b.get('text')]
83|                note = '\n\n'.join(chunks) if chunks else final.get('result', '')
84|                with paths['note'].open('x') as handle:
85|                    handle.write(note.rstrip() + '\n')
86|                record['status'] = 'REVIEW_RETURNED_UNVERIFIED'
87|        except (ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, OSError) as exc:
88|            record['parse_error'] = str(exc)
89|    record['finished_at'] = clock()
90|    record['duration_seconds'] = time.monotonic() - begin
91|    write_new(paths['receipt'], record)
92|    print(json.dumps(record, indent=2), flush=True)
93|    return 0 if record['status'] == 'REVIEW_RETURNED_UNVERIFIED' else 1
